*Aoyang Fang,Yifan Yang,Jin'ao Shang,Qisheng Lu,Junjielung Xu,Rui Wang,Songhan Zhang,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Yu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有RCA数据集仅标注根因，忽略了从根因到症状的因果传播路径，导致LLM智能体可退化为简单模式匹配。本文提出PAVE协议，通过故障注入中的已知干预前向重构因果路径，构建带步骤级因果标注的跨系统RCA基准OpenRCA 2.0（500实例）。评估11个前沿LLM发现，精确恢复根因集平均仅20.7%；进一步分析揭示"无根诊断"问题：智能体在76.0%案例中找到至少一个正确根因服务，但仅有61.5%能将其通过验证的因果路径与观察症状关联。仅评估结果会隐藏此失效模式，步骤级因果真值是实现可信LLM RCA智能体的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA数据集仅标注根因，缺乏从根因到症状的因果传播路径，使得任务退化为简单的模式匹配，无法真正评估LLM智能体的多步推理和因果理解能力。

Method: 提出PAVE步骤级标注协议，利用故障注入中的已知干预信息，通过前向验证机制（从原因到效应推理）重构因果传播路径，构建OpenRCA 2.0基准数据集。

Result: 11个前沿LLM精确恢复根因集平均仅20.7%；智能体在76.0%案例中找到至少一个正确根因服务，但仅61.5%能将其通过已验证因果路径关联到症状，揭示"无根诊断"问题。

Conclusion: 仅评估根因结果会隐藏智能体缺乏因果路径验证的失效模式，步骤级因果真值是实现可信LLM RCA智能体的关键组件。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) poses a holistic test of LLM agentic capabilities, such as long-context understanding, multi-step reasoning, and tool use. However, existing datasets suffer from a fundamental gap: they label only the root cause, not the propagation path connecting it to the observed symptom, which largely simplifies the task to naive pattern matching. To support rigorous evaluation, we introduce PAVE, a step-wise labeling protocol that leverages known interventions from fault injection to reconstruct causal propagation paths. The mechanism is forward verification: reasoning from cause to effect rather than inferring backward from symptoms. Applying PAVE yields OpenRCA 2.0 (500 instances), the first cross-system RCA benchmark with step-wise causal annotations for LLM agents. Across 11 frontier LLMs, recovering the exact root-cause set succeeds in only 20.7% of cases on average. To locate where this difficulty lies, we relax the criterion and find what we call the ungrounded diagnosis: agents identify at least one correct root-cause service in 76.0% of cases, but ground that service in a verified causal propagation path to the observed symptom in only 61.5%. Outcome-only evaluation hides this failure mode; step-wise causal ground truth is the missing piece for trustworthy LLM-based RCA agents.

</details>


### [177] [TOPS: First-Principles Visual Token Pruning via Constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets for Efficient MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27161)
*Tinghao Wang,Yichen Guo,Rui Huang,Zheng Lu,Qizhe Zhang,Chenxi Li,Yuan Zhang,Jiajun Cao,Zhirong Shen,Yaosong Du,Guangyan Gan,Wenya Wang,Lin William Cong,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，将视觉令牌剪枝形式化为构建最优保留集（TOPS），通过信息论分析识别出任务相关性、信息覆盖度和语义多样性三个基本原则，提出一种免训练、与模型无关的剪枝模块。在7个MLLM骨干和14个基准上，TOPS优于先前方法，在LLaVA-NeXT上移除77.8%视觉令牌仍可保持甚至提升性能，表明剪枝冗余令牌可缓解幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽有强大推理能力，但大量视觉令牌导致计算开销高昂。现有视觉令牌剪枝方法存在不足：基于注意力的准则会保留冗余令牌，基于多样性的准则忽视用户指令，多准则组合方法缺乏对剪枝目标的规范化表述。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，通过自上而下的信息论分析，识别出任务相关性（Task Relevance）、信息覆盖度（Information Coverage）、语义多样性（Semantic Diversity）三个基本原则，并据此提出TOPS（Token Optimal Preservation Sets），一种免训练、与模型无关的剪枝模块。

Result: 在7个MLLM骨干和14个基准上，TOPS在多种剪枝设置下均优于先前方法。在LLaVA-NeXT上，TOPS移除77.8%视觉令牌后，7B和13B模型分别保持了100.0%和100.6%的性能，表明剪枝冗余令牌有时可缓解幻觉。

Conclusion: TOPS通过信息论指导的视觉令牌剪枝，在大幅降低计算开销的同时保持甚至提升模型性能，为未来轻量级MLLM设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but their efficiency is limited by the large number of visual tokens, which introduces substantial computational overhead. Visual token pruning offers a natural solution, yet existing methods are imperfect: attention-based criteria tend to retain redundant tokens, while diversity-based criteria are often agnostic to user instructions. Even methods that combine multiple criteria still lack a principled formulation of the intrinsic objective of token pruning. In this paper, we revisit visual token pruning from a first-principles perspective and formulate it as constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets. Through a top-down information-theoretic analysis, we identify three fundamental principles for effective token selection: Task Relevance, Information Coverage, and Semantic Diversity. Based on these principles, we propose TOPS, a training-free and model-agnostic pruning module that can be applied to various MLLMs. Extensive experiments on 7 MLLM backbones and 14 benchmarks demonstrate that TOPS outperforms prior methods under diverse pruning settings. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, TOPS removes 77.8% of visual tokens while preserving 100.0% and 100.6% performance on its 7B and 13B models, respectively, suggesting that pruning redundant visual tokens can sometimes mitigate hallucination and inspire future lightweight MLLM design.

</details>


### [178] [A Process Harness for Uplifting Legacy Workflows to Agentic BPM: Design and Realization in CUGA FLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27188)
*Fabiana Fournier,Lior Limonad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"流程驾驭器"(process harness)机制，通过在确定性工作流引擎外围添加受策略约束的智能体层，实现遗留工作流向Agentic BPM的演进。作者定义了TDF模型（任务-决策-流模型），包含三种策略治理的智能体类型（TaskAgent、DecisionAgent、FlowAgent），并基于CUGA FLO实现该模型，在贷款审批流程中验证了所有智能体类型和钩子驱动的监管覆盖机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留工作流引擎难以适应Agentic BPM对推理、适应和监管的需求，而完全替换底层工作流引擎成本高昂且风险巨大。亟需一种机制在不替换现有引擎的前提下，为确定性工作流注入智能体能力。

Method: 提出"流程驾驭器"架构，在确定性工作流引擎外部放置策略治理的智能体层。定义TDF模型（数据模式+执行语义），将LLM推理分解为三种策略治理的智能体类型：TaskAgent（知识密集型任务执行）、DecisionAgent（按需网关路由）、FlowAgent（通过钩子机制实现运行时流适应）。每个智能体在流程FRAME（聚合策略集）的显式策略范围内推理。CUGA FLO是该模型的设计与实现。

Result: 在贷款审批流程上成功演示了三种智能体类型和钩子驱动的监管覆盖机制。流程驾驭器独特地调和了命令性要求（通过确定性工作流执行实现结构合规）与规范性要求（通过策略框架化的智能体自主性在指定控制点实现）之间的矛盾。

Conclusion: 流程驾驭器是实现遗留工作流向Agentic BPM演进的有效机制，在不替换底层引擎的前提下，通过策略治理的智能体层为确定性工作流注入推理、适应和监管能力，成功调和了结构合规与智能自主性之间的矛盾。

Abstract: We introduce the process harness, a new mechanism for uplifting legacy workflows into Agentic Business Process Management (Agentic BPM) without replacing the underlying workflow engine. A process harness places a policy-governed agentic layer around a deterministic workflow engine, intercepting designated control points to contribute reasoning, adaptation, and oversight while the engine retains structural authority over the process. To define the process harness rigorously, we develop the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, specifying both its data schema and its execution semantics. TDF decomposes LLM reasoning across three policy-governed agent types: a TaskAgent for knowledge-intensive task execution, a DecisionAgent for per-case gateway routing, and a FlowAgent that governs runtime flow adaptation through a principled hook mechanism. Each agent reasons within an explicit policy drawn from the process FRAME, the aggregate policy set governing all LLM calls in the system. We then present CUGA FLO as the design and implementation realization of the TDF model, and demonstrate it on a loan approval workflow that exercises all three agent types and hook-driven regulatory override. The process harness uniquely reconciles imperative requirements, realized through deterministic workflow execution that enforces structural compliance, with normative requirements, realized through policy-framed agentic autonomy invoked at designated control points wherever the process demands it.

</details>


### [179] [Vulnerability of Natural Language Classifiers to Evolutionary Generated Adversarial Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27215)
*Manjinder Singh,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Mohamed Elawady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAversary，一种基于混合遗传算法的黑盒对抗攻击方法，利用GloVe词嵌入进行词替换（变异操作），在多个基准数据集上将模型准确率从76.8%大幅降至5.8%，优于BAE和A2T攻击方法，但扰动词数量约为对比方法的近两倍，语义相似度略低，运行时间增加约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在NLP领域易受对抗样本攻击，现有攻击方法虽能通过替换语义相似的token来欺骗模型，但往往需要一定程度的模型内部结构访问。本文旨在提出一种仅需模型logit输出的黑盒对抗攻击方法，以降低攻击门槛并提升攻击效果。

Method: 提出GAversary，一种混合遗传算法，将目标模型视为黑盒，仅利用其输出的logit值指导搜索；使用GloVe词嵌入作为变异算子来生成语义相似的词替换，提高对抗样本的语义相似性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，GAversary显著降低了目标模型的测试准确率（最佳情况从76.8%降至5.8%），优于BAE（降至27.6%）和A2T攻击；但扰动词数量约为对比方法的近两倍，语义相似度略低，运行时间增加约5%。

Conclusion: GAversary作为一种黑盒对抗攻击方法，在攻击效果上优于现有方法，但存在扰动词数量较多、语义相似度略低和运行时间增加的权衡。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance across various fields but remain vulnerable to adversarial inputs, particularly in NLP, where such attacks can have significant real-world consequences. Adversarial attacks often involve small, semantically similar token replacements to fool NLP models, and recent methods have become more precise by targeting specific vulnerable words, often by exploiting some level of access to the model's internal structure. This paper proposes GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm (GA) to generate adversarial attacks on natural language models. The GA is able to treat the target model as a black box, requiring only the logit value output by the model to guide the search. GAversary differs from GAs previously proposed for this problem by using GloVe embeddings to propose word replacements (the mutation operator) to improve the semantic similarity of the adversarial examples. GAversary is applied to several benchmark data sets and well-known target models. GAversary is able to substantially reduce the target model's accuracy on test data compared to the BAE and A2T attacks compared against (in the best case, reducing a 76.8% accuracy to 5.8%, compared to BAE's 27.6%). The trade-off is that GAversary perturbs just under twice as many words as the other two methods, with a slightly lower semantic similarity to the original text and around a 5% increase in run-time.

</details>


### [180] [Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27226)
*Sangwoo Cho,Kushal Chawla,Pengshan Cai,Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BINEVAL 提出将评估标准分解为原子化的二元问题，通过LLM逐问题回答生成可解释的多维度评分，在多个基准上匹配或超越现有方法，并支持提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出评估是NLP的瓶颈：人工评估昂贵缓慢、词汇度量与人工判断相关性差、整体性LLM评估器产生难以调试的不透明分数。

Method: 将评估标准分解为原子二元问题，元提示生成细粒度评估问题，LLM独立回答每个问题，聚合判据为可解释的多维分数。

Result: 在SummEval、Topical-Chat、QAGS上匹配或超越UniEval和G-Eval等基线，尤其在事实一致性基准QAGS上表现优异；更好匹配人类分数分布，避免天花板效应；支持提示迭代优化。

Conclusion: BINEVAL是一个任务无关、无需训练、可解释的评估框架，兼具强实证性能与实用诊断及优化价值。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.

</details>


### [181] [EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27277)
*Junwei Luo,Shuai Yuan,Zhenya Yang,Yansheng Li,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EO-WM，一种用于多光谱地球观测预测的视频扩散Transformer模型。该模型通过物理信息条件框架，将气象强迫分解为气候基线、天气异常和累积物理应力信号，分别通过不同路径进行条件化处理，以更好地捕捉天气驱动下的地表动态变化。同时，引入极端夏季基准和季节匹配对基准两个诊断基准来评估天气响应行为。实验表明，EO-WM在NDVI下降幅度误差上降低5.63%，方向命中率提升7.80%，并在标准像素级指标上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测预测方法存在三方面不足：1) 确定性模型将不确定性压缩为单一预测；2) 扩散方法将天气变量视为无差异的条件信号；3) 现有基准主要关注重建精度，而非预测是否对天气强迫变化做出正确响应。本文旨在将EO预测视为一个部分观测、天气驱动的世界建模问题，并开发能更好捕捉这一设定的方法。

Method: 提出EO-WM，一个视频扩散Transformer模型。核心创新是物理信息条件框架：将气象强迫分解为气候基线、天气异常和累积物理应力信号。通过不同条件路径分离基线与异常，并随时间累积异常强迫以捕捉持续的热和干旱应力。此外，引入两个诊断基准：极端夏季基准（评估极端天气下植被退化的严重程度感知预测）和季节匹配对基准（测试天气强迫变化下的响应 fidelity）。

Result: EO-WM在NDVI下降幅度误差上相对降低5.63%，方向命中率相对提升7.80%，同时在标准像素级指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过物理信息条件框架和诊断基准，EO-WM有效改进了地球观测预测对天气强迫变化的响应能力，特别是在极端天气场景下对植被退化程度的预测更为准确。模型和基准将开源。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.

</details>


### [182] [Simulation-based inference for rapid Bayesian parameter estimation in epidemiological models: a comparison with MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27286)
*Alina Bazarova,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Henrik Zunker,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Torben Heinsohn,Wolfgang Wiechert,Katharina Noeh,Stefan Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了将基于仿真的推断（SBI）与神经后验估计相结合，作为机制性流行病学模型（SECIR）贝叶斯校准的可扩展替代方案，利用德国2020年COVID-19 ICU入住数据，证明SBI在31天和201天推断窗口下均能获得与MCMC高度一致的后验分布，同时大幅降低计算时间（31天问题从~1000秒降至~60-70秒，201天问题从>19,000秒降至~157秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制性流行病学模型的贝叶斯校准常用MCMC，但对于高维非线性系统和重复近实时分析，MCMC计算成本高昂，亟需更高效的替代方法。

Method: 采用神经后验估计的SBI方法，以MCMC为基准，在31天推断窗口和201天多传播变化点重建问题中，使用Wasserstein距离和KL散度定量评估后验一致性，并结合后验预测检查进行验证。

Result: 31天窗口下，SBI恢复的后验分布与MCMC高度一致，准确复现ICU轨迹；201天设置下，尽管不确定性增加，SBI仍保留主导后验结构。计算速度上，SBI在单GPU上仅需60-70秒（31天）和157秒（201天），远低于MCMC的~1000秒和>19,000秒。

Conclusion: SBI为机制性流行病学模型的贝叶斯校准提供了快速且计算高效的框架，适用于重复近实时推断和快速疫情分析。

Abstract: Mechanistic epidemiological models are widely used to support infectious disease forecasting and public-health decision making. Bayesian calibration of such models is commonly performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), which can become computationally expensive for high-dimensional nonlinear systems and repeated near-real-time analyses. Here, we investigate simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a scalable alternative for Bayesian calibration of a mechanistic SECIR epidemiological model using COVID-19 intensive care unit (ICU) occupancy data from Germany during 2020. We compared SBI and MCMC across multiple epidemic phases using both 31-day inference windows and a substantially more challenging 201-day reconstruction problem involving multiple transmission change points. Posterior agreement was evaluated quantitatively using Wasserstein distances and Kullback-Leibler divergences together with posterior predictive checks. Across the 31-day windows, SBI recovered posterior distributions in strong agreement with MCMC while accurately reproducing observed ICU trajectories. In the 201-day setting, SBI preserved the dominant posterior structure despite increased uncertainty. SBI, by combining CPU and GPU resources, substantially reduced computational runtime compared with MCMC, which was restricted to running on CPUs. Whereas MCMC required approximately 1000 seconds for the 31-day inference problems, SBI achieved comparable posterior and predictive performance in approximately 60-70 seconds on a single GPU. For the 201-day inference problem, SBI required an average of 157 seconds, while the MCMC runs took over 19,000 seconds. Our results demonstrate that SBI provides a rapid and computationally efficient framework for Bayesian calibration of mechanistic epidemiological models, supporting repeated near-real-time inference and rapid outbreak analysis.

</details>


### [183] [Prompt Injection in Automated Résumé Screening with Large Language Models: Single and Multi-Injection Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27287)
*Preet Baxi,Jiannan Xu,Jane Yi Jiang,Stefanus Jasin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提示注入对LLM简历筛选的影响：当候选人质量同质且少数人使用注入时有效，但随使用人数增多效果消失；在质量异质时效果平均较弱，但可能让低质量候选人超越高质量候选人，引发公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于简历筛选和排序，候选人可能策略性地操纵算法招聘系统。作者研究提示注入（在简历中嵌入自我推销文本）是否能影响LLM评估，以及其在不同情境下的有效性和公平性影响。

Method: 通过控制实验，在候选人质量同质/异质、提示注入使用比例不同的条件下，测试提示注入对LLM简历排序结果的影响。

Result: 1）候选人质量同质且少数人注入时，提示注入可靠提升排序；2）随注入人数增多，效果迅速减弱直至失效；3）质量异质时平均效果较弱，但偶尔可使低质量候选人超越高质量候选人。

Conclusion: LLM简历筛选在操纵罕见且候选人质量差异小时最脆弱。提示注入引发公平性担忧，需警惕算法招聘系统的安全漏洞。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to screen and rank job applicants, creating incentives for candidates to strategically manipulate algorithmic hiring systems. We study prompt injection in automated résumé screening, defined as subtle self-promotional text that introduces no new qualifications but is designed to influence LLM evaluations. Using controlled experiments, we show that prompt injection reliably improves applicant rankings when résumé quality is homogeneous and few candidates inject. However, its effectiveness rapidly diminishes as more candidates inject, collapsing when manipulation becomes widespread. When candidate quality is heterogeneous, prompt injection is less effective on average, but can occasionally allow lower-quality candidates to outrank higher-quality ones, raising fairness concerns. Overall, LLM-based screening is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and candidate quality differences are small. Code and resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26

</details>


### [184] [When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27288)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模型LLM系统（路由、投票、级联等）的精度增益存在理论上限：所有模型同时出错的比率β。常用诊断指标ρ无法识别β，且β在实际中常被低估（如数学任务中低估约2.5倍）。在可检查任务中，无强路由信号时，组合模型很难超越单一最佳模型，增益源于模型在不同问题上的互补失败而非增加数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示多模型LLM系统精度增益的真实上限，指出学术界常用的平均成对错误相关性ρ无法捕捉所有模型同时出错的比率β，从而挑战了现有评估方法的有效性。

Method: 1. 理论推导：证明对于输出为单模型答案的策略，精度上限为1-β（β为所有模型同时出错率）。2. 对比ρ与β：证明相同边际分布和成对相关性的错误定律可以有不同的全错率。3. 使用Clopper-Pearson界限给出有限样本证书。4. 在67个模型（21个提供商）上进行实证分析，使用四分相关校准的单因子模型、高斯copula模型等，评估开放数学、执行评分代码、GPQA-Diamond自由回答等任务。

Result: 1. 开放数学任务：观测β=0.052，而67模型高斯copula预测β=0.023，低估约2.5倍（90%置信区间1.7-3.4，k=17）。2. 代码任务：β=0.079。3. GPQA-Diamond自由回答：β=0.127，五裁判面板kappa=0.73-0.92，共失败源于答案格式而非学科。4. 同质量下，低ρ异质集成优于高ρ Self-MoA。5. 可检查任务中，无强查询级路由信号时，组合模型很少超越单一最佳模型。

Conclusion: 多模型系统的增益存在严格的理论上限β（全错率），而常用指标ρ无法刻画这一关键量。实际中β常被低估，增益的真正来源是模型在不同问题上互补失败，而非简单增加模型数量。在可检查任务中，缺乏强路由信号时，组合模型难以超越最佳单一模型。

Abstract: Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical marginals and pairwise correlations can have different all-wrong rates. A Clopper-Pearson bound on beta gives a finite-sample certificate on the largest gain any router, vote, or cascade could deliver before training a router.
  Across 67 models from 21 providers, a tetrachoric-calibrated single-factor model still underprices the all-wrong tail: on open-ended mathematics, observed beta is 0.052 versus 0.023 under the full 67-model Gaussian copula, about 2.5 times underpricing, with 90 percent CI 1.7 to 3.4 and k equals 17. The effect recurs on execution-graded code, where beta is 0.079. Re-asking the same GPQA-Diamond questions in free-response rather than multiple-choice form reopens the tail, with beta 0.127 and a five-judge panel with kappa 0.73 to 0.92, locating co-failure in answer format rather than subject. At matched quality, low-rho heterogeneous ensembles beat high-rho Self-MoA, but on checkable tasks in our pool, combining models rarely beats the single best model without a strong query-level routing signal. Gains come from models failing on different questions, not from adding more models.

</details>


### [185] [Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27334)
*Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor,Hiroko H. Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言数字孪生的框架，利用大语言模型结合文体特征和元数据模拟老年人对话行为，并通过多头条件变分自编码器评估重建质量和认知得分，在I-CONECT数据集上验证了个性化认知健康监测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻度认知障碍（MCI）的早期检测仍具挑战，而语言和对话模式可作为非侵入性生物标志物，因此需要一种可扩展、个性化的认知健康监测方法。

Method: 构建语言数字孪生框架：利用LLM结合文体线索和上下文元数据模拟老年人对话行为；提出多头条件变分自编码器（cVAE）联合评估重建保真度和认知分数预测。

Result: 在I-CONECT数据集上，数字孪生保留了身份特定特征，重建误差和MoCA预测误差与真实数据相当，且优于基线GPT生成响应。

Conclusion: 语言数字孪生作为一种可扩展、非侵入性的方法，在个性化和持续认知健康监测方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [The Role of Input Dimensionality in the Emergence and Targeted Control of Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26207)
*Nasrin Malekzadeh Goradel,Niccolo Pancino,Yaser Gholizade Atani,Benedetta Tondi,Giovanni Bellettini,Mauro Barni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了输入维度在对抗样本产生与目标攻击控制中的作用。通过分析度量集中理论的局限、跨维度层次化图像数据集的广泛实验，发现高输入维度会使对抗样本更容易构造，且目标攻击与无目标攻击所需的扰动差距会随维度升高而缩小。研究确认高输入维度是对抗样本出现的基础因素，但其根源是高维几何与数据分布的相互作用还是深度网络架构特性，仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论工作尝试用高维几何性质解释深度神经网络的对抗脆弱性，但这些理论假设缺乏系统的实证检验，相关经验证据有限。作者旨在通过系统研究填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 分析现有基于度量集中理论框架的适用范围与局限性；2) 在跨层次图像数据集（覆盖不同输入维度）及多种神经网络架构上进行大规模实证评估；3) 提供理论论证说明高维几何中目标攻击相比无目标攻击仅需有限额外扰动；4) 通过实验验证目标攻击与无目标攻击扰动差距随维度增加而缩小。

Result: 1) 真实图像类别表现出强经验局域性，超出度量集中理论的典型假设；2) 对抗样本随输入维度升高而更易构造；3) 目标攻击与无目标攻击所需的扰动差距很小，且随维度升高进一步缩小。

Conclusion: 高输入维度是对抗样本出现和目标攻击可控性的基础因素，但其根本机制（高维几何与数据分布的相互作用 vs. 深度网络架构特性）尚不明确。

Abstract: Several theoretical works have tried to explain the adversarial vulnerability of deep neural networks through properties of high-dimensional geometry. However, the assumptions underlying these works are rarely examined empirically, and systematic evidence remains limited. In this work, we present a systematic study of the role of input dimensionality in both the emergence and the targeted control of adversarial examples. We first analyse the scope and limitations of existing theoretical frameworks based on concentration of measure, showing that real image classes exhibit strong empirical localization, beyond what such theories typically assume. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation across hierarchical image datasets spanning a wide range of input dimensionalities and diverse neural architectures. Our results consistently show that adversarial examples become easier to construct as dimensionality increases. We also investigate how input dimensionality affects the additional difficulty of crafting targeted adversarial examples. In particular, we provide theoretical arguments showing that high-dimensional geometry implies that enforcing a specific target label entails only a limited additional distortion compared to untargeted attacks. We corroborate this insight through extensive experiments, demonstrating that the gap between targeted and untargeted perturbations remains small and further narrows as input dimensionality increases. While, taken together, our findings establish high input dimensionality as a fundamental factor underlying the emergence and targeted control of adversarial examples, whether this phenomenon primarily arises from the interplay between high-dimensional geometry and data distributions or from the architectural properties of deep neural networks remains an open question.

</details>


### [187] [A probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26457)
*Daniel Corrales,David Ríos Insua*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于在线测试时自适应问题的概率框架，基于状态空间建模架构，统一描述了参数学习、参数时间演化、先验调优和预测过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在测试时自适应问题中，模型在训练数据和测试数据分布可能存在差异的情况下，需要在测试阶段仅利用未标注数据进行自适应，现有方法缺乏统一的概率框架来系统地刻画这一过程中的参数学习、演化和预测。

Method: 采用状态空间建模架构，将在线测试时自适应问题建模为概率框架，涵盖参数学习、参数随时间的演化、先验调优和预测四个核心环节。

Result: 所提出的概率框架能够统一刻画在线测试时自适应中的关键组件，为处理训练-测试分布偏移提供了系统性的理论支撑。

Conclusion: 该概率框架为在线测试时自适应问题提供了一种统一的理论视角和方法论基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation problems. In them, a model is trained on labeled data but must adapt to unlabeled data at test time under the assumption that training and test distributions potentially differ, that is, there might have been a distributional shift. The framework is based on a state-space modelling architecture from which parameter learning, parameter time evolution, prior tuning, and prediction can be characterized.

</details>


### [188] [XMSE-Aware Adaptive Empirical Bayes Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26975)
*Minghao Chen,Jiale Zheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于XMSE感知的混合估计器，在经验贝叶斯（EB）收缩估计和最大似然估计（ML）之间进行自适应插值。当核函数与真实参数匹配良好时保留EB的正则化优势，在核函数误设时退回到ML估计。该方法具有闭合形式的神谕混合权重、一致的插件实现以及二阶神谕遗憾率，在仿真和公开基准数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验贝叶斯（EB）估计器虽然在渐近风险一阶意义上匹配最大似然估计（ML），但在二阶分析中，当核函数与真实参数对齐不良时，EB估计的过量均方误差（XMSE）可能比ML更差。本文旨在将这种诊断性发现转化为设计原则，提出一种能够自适应地在EB和ML之间权衡的混合估计方法。

Method: 提出XMSE感知的混合估计器，在ML和EB收缩估计之间线性插值。固定权重下的XMSE是标量二次型，可得到闭合形式的最优神谕混合权重。采用基于有限样本XMSE近似的插件实现，并证明了该实现的一致性及其二阶神谕遗憾率。此外，将遗憾界推广到选定权重下的固定权重风险曲线，引入阈值边界规则，并扩展至紧致核族及有限/增长核字典的高概率神谕界。

Result: 在有限脉冲响应仿真中，以SURE调优、硬选择和迹校正基线作为对照，并在公开Silverbox和Cascaded Tanks基准数据集上验证。结果表明，所提估计器在正则化有益时保留了大部分收益，在核函数误设时退回到ML，且在基准数据集上给出了有限样本分析结果。

Conclusion: XMSE感知的混合估计器能够自适应地在EB收缩和ML之间平衡，有效解决了EB在核函数误设下性能退化的问题，同时保留了正则化带来的优势。理论保证了二阶神谕最优性，实验验证了实际有效性。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) estimators can match the first-order asymptotic risk of maximum likelihood (ML) while behaving very differently at second order: recent excess mean squared error (XMSE) analysis shows that kernel-based EB estimation may be worse than ML when the kernel is poorly aligned with the true parameter. This paper turns that diagnostic into a design principle. We propose an XMSE-aware mixed estimator that interpolates between ML and EB shrinkage. Its fixed-weight XMSE is a scalar quadratic, yielding a closed-form oracle mixing weight that is no worse than both ML and the base EB estimator at the XMSE scale. A plug-in implementation based on finite-sample XMSE approximations is proved consistent, with a second-order oracle regret rate for an interior oracle weight. We further establish a transfer of the regret bound to the fixed-weight risk curve evaluated at the selected weight, a thresholded boundary rule, and extensions to compact kernel families and to finite and growing kernel dictionaries with high-probability oracle bounds. Finite impulse response simulations with SURE-tuned, hard-selection, and trace-corrected baselines, together with the public Silverbox and Cascaded Tanks benchmarks, show that the proposed estimator retains most of the benefit of regularization when it is helpful and retreats toward ML under kernel misspecification, with an identified finite-de analyzed on the benchmarks.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Global Divergences: A Local-Mass Perspective on Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27090)
*Hanli Xu,Fengxiang He,Sarat Moka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯推断中KL散度和ELBO等全局目标函数无法捕捉的局部质量行为，提出了两个数学工具：用于记录局部质量多项式和对数衰减尺度的质量指数（Mass Index），以及可在奇异成分存在时定义的正则化扩展KL散度（RE-KL）。通过这些工具揭示了贝叶斯更新如何改变局部质量，并证明了局部小球质量的绝对、相对和方向性不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯推断方法广泛使用KL散度、ELBO等全局目标函数来度量分布差异，但这些全局目标无法捕捉分布的局部质量行为（如参数空间特定点附近的概率质量分布）。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，建立局部质量行为的理论框架。

Method: 提出了两个数学工具：(1) 质量指数（Mass Index），用于记录局部质量的多项式和对数衰减尺度；(2) 正则化扩展KL散度（RE-KL），一种可在奇异成分存在时定义的局部化散度。利用这些工具分析贝叶斯更新如何改变局部质量，并证明局部RE-KL下的各种不等式。

Result: 揭示了贝叶斯更新改变局部质量的两种机制：(1) 幂对数似然因子显式地改变局部尺度；(2) 依赖于参数的支持集或其平滑软化通过参数值附近剩余的质量量改变局部尺度。证明了局部小球质量在两种KL方向下的绝对、相对和方向性不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作为局部质量行为提供了系统的局部理论解释，补充了全局目标函数无法覆盖的视角。实验验证了理论结果，并开源了代码。

Abstract: Global objectives, such as KL divergence and ELBO, are widely used in Bayesian inference for measuring distributional discrepancy. This paper studies their local-mass behaviour that is not directly captured by such objectives. We introduce and use two mathematical tools: (1) Mass Index for recording the polynomial and logarithmic decay scales of local mass, and (2) regularised extended KL (RE-KL), a set-localised divergence that can be formulated in the presence of singular components. Mass Indices help characterise how Bayesian updating changes local mass: (1) power-log likelihood factors shift it explicitly, and (2) parameter-dependent supports, or their smooth softenings, may change the local scale through the amount of mass that remains near the parameter value. Using local RE-KL, we prove absolute, relative, and directional inequalities for comparing local small-ball masses under the two KL directions. Together, these results provide a local theoretical account of local mass behaviour. Experiments provide controlled illustrations of the local behaviour. Code is available at https://github.com/Forsythia0604/Local-Mass-Framework.

</details>


### [190] [Ribbon: Scalable Approximation and Robust Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27269)
*Graham Gibson,John Tipton,Kellin Rumsey,Natalie Klein*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Ribbon是一种可扩展的贝叶斯自助法不确定性量化近似方法，通过影响函数线性化避免了重复模型重拟合的计算开销，在理论上与拉普拉斯近似和鲁棒三明治协方差等价，实验中表现出良好的校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂、高维或错误设定的模型中，可靠量化预测不确定性困难。完全贝叶斯方法和自助重抽样方法需要后验采样或重复模型重拟合，计算成本过高，难以应用于现代机器学习模型。

Method: Ribbon使用影响函数线性化（influence-function linearization）在单个已拟合模型周围对狄利克雷重加权自助法不确定性进行近似，将重复重拟合替换为事后线性代数运算。通过调节浓度参数，得到一族可校准的狄利克雷重加权不确定性估计。

Result: 在合成回归、MNIST分类和California Housing基准上，Ribbon在避免重复模型训练的同时提供了具有竞争力的预测性能和更好的校准效果。理论上，在正确似然设定下渐近等价于平坦先验拉普拉斯近似，在模型错误设定下恢复鲁棒三明治协方差。

Conclusion: Ribbon通过影响函数线性化近似贝叶斯自助法的不确定性量化，在避免重复模型训练的同时提供了校准良好的预测不确定性估计，并在理论上证明了与拉普拉斯近似和鲁棒三明治协方差的一致性。

Abstract: Reliably quantifying predictive uncertainty is difficult for complex, high-dimensional, or misspecified models. Both fully Bayesian and bootstrap resampling methods provide principled uncertainty estimates but are often too expensive for modern machine-learning models because they require posterior sampling or repeated model refitting. We introduce Ribbon, a scalable approximation to Dirichlet-reweighted bootstrap uncertainty. Ribbon replaces repeated refitting with an influence-function linearization around a single fitted model, preserving the first-order data-reweighting structure of the Bayesian bootstrap while requiring only post-hoc linear algebra. Ribbon approximates the Bayesian-bootstrap or weighted-likelihood-bootstrap refitting target. With a general concentration parameter, Ribbon gives a calibrated Dirichlet-reweighting family whose uncertainty scale can be tuned on validation data. We show that Ribbon is asymptotically equivalent to a flat-prior Laplace approximation under correct likelihood specification and recovers the robust sandwich covariance under misspecification. Across synthetic regression, MNIST classification, and California Housing benchmarks, Ribbon provides competitive predictive performance and improved calibration in several settings while avoiding repeated model retraining.

</details>


### [191] [When are likely answers right? On Sequence Probability and Correctness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27359)
*Johannes Zenn,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了序列概率（给定提示下续写内容的条件概率）与生成正确性之间的关系，发现在固定数据集内跨问答对时，高序列概率往往预测正确性，但这一关系不能推广到解码决策层面：通过改变超参数或解码方法来提高序列概率并不能可靠地提升准确率，且对同一提示的多次响应而言，序列概率并非正确性的良好指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多解码方法依赖于将概率质量转移到模型更可能输出的内容上，但序列概率（条件概率）与正确性之间的对齐关系尚未被系统量化。本文旨在澄清何时解码可以预期提升正确性，何时不能。

Method: 在四个层面（解码方法间、方法内超参数间、数据集内问答对间、同一提示的多次响应间）对序列概率与正确性的关系进行量化分析，涵盖多种解码方法、模型和基准测试。

Result: 1) 数据集内跨问答对时，高序列概率通常预测正确性；2) 通过改变超参数或解码方法提高序列概率并不能可靠提升准确率；3) 对同一提示的多次响应，序列概率不是正确性的良好指标。

Conclusion: 这些发现厘清了何时解码能及不能提升正确性，为解码方法、自一致性（self-consistency）和无验证器自我改进（verifier-free self-improvement）提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Many decoding methods for large language models can be understood as shifting probability mass toward outputs that are more likely under the model, either locally at the token level or globally at the sequence level. Therefore, their success depends on a fundamental question: when does sequence probability, that is, the conditional probability of a continuation given a prompt, actually align with correctness? In this paper, we set out to quantify this relationship across decoding methods, models, and benchmarks at four levels: across decoding methods, across hyperparameters within a method, across prompt-answer pairs within a dataset, and across repeated responses to the same prompt. We find that higher sequence probability is often predictive of correctness across prompt-answer pairs within a fixed dataset. However, this relationship does not generally transfer to decoding decisions: increasing sequence probability by changing hyperparameters or methods does not reliably improve accuracy. Further, sequence probability is not a good indicator of correctness for responses to the same prompt. These findings clarify when decoding can and cannot be expected to improve correctness, and provide practical guidance for decoding, self-consistency, and verifier-free self-improvement.

</details>
